# Magma Cube Minion
Aliases: Magma Cream Minion, Magma Cube Minion I, Magma Cube Minion II, Magma Cube Minion III, Magma Cube Minion IV, Magma Cube Minion IX, Magma Cube Minion V, Magma Cube Minion VI, Magma Cube Minion VII, Magma Cube Minion VIII, Magma Cube Minion X, Magma Cube Minion XI, Magma Cube Minion XII
Categories: Minions, Craftable items, Magma Cream Collection
[Magma Cube Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Magma Cream I (1) ([[Magma Cream]])
Category: [[Combat]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1–3x [[Magma Cream]]
Upgrade with: [[Magma Cream]] [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]
[Metadata]
ID: MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_1; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_2; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_3; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_4; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_5; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_6; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_7; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_8; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_9; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_10; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_11; MAGMA_CUBE_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and slaying Magma Cubes!; Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 32s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Magma Cube Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Magma Cube (Minion Mob)|Magma Cubes]] and collects [[Magma Cream]]. Magma Cube Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Magma Cube|Magma Cube I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Magma Cream]]|+0.2 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 8 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 512 [[Coins]]|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 2|[[Magma Cream]]|+0.2 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 8 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 512 [[Coins]]|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 2|[[Magma Cream]]|+0.2 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 8 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 512 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Magma Cream]]|[[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|+32 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 1,280 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 81,920 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Magma Cream]]|[[Whipped Magma Cream]]|+5,120 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 204,800 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 13,107,200 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
[[Magma Cube (Minion Mob)|Magma Cubes]] spawned by a Magma Cube Minion come in 4 sizes: small, medium, semi-large, and large. For the minion to spawn a magma cube, there has to be enough space for the largest magma cube, which is 3x3x3.
### Minion Modifiers
Some [[Minions|Minion Modifiers]] can only be applied to a limited selection of [[Minions]]. The following of which can be applied to a Magma Cube Minion.

A Melon Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants [[Melon Seeds]] and collects [[Melon Slice|Melon Slices]]. Melon Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Melon Slice|Melon Slice I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 5; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Melon Slice]]|+0.1 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 2 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 128 [[Coins]]|
|[[Compactor]]|9x [[Melon Slice]]|[[Melon]]|+0.9 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 2 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 128 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Melon Slice]]|[[Enchanted Melon Slice]]|+16 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 320 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 20,480 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Melon Slice]]|[[Enchanted Melon]]|+2,560 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 320 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 20,480 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
When a Melon Minion has planted all the [[Melon Seeds]], it will not have to perform more planting actions as [[Melon|Melons]] will grown automatically before each harvest. This only happens while the [[Private Island]] is loaded (see #Bugs).

## Bugs
- Offline calculations generate [[Melon Slice|Melon Slices]] twice as slow due to the simulation assuming a replant step is necessary after each harvest.
## Trivia
- Melon Minions produce the second most [[Farming|Farming XP]] per day of any farming minion, being beaten by [[Cactus Minion|Cactus Minions]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Melon Minion Tier I-XI.|
|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Buffed time between action of Melon Minion by approximately 20% each level.|
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added Tier XII Melon Minion.|
|Oct 24, 2023|0.19.6|Fixed Melon Minions not giving the correct amount of collection from Melons ([[Melon]]).|

## Bugs
- [[Mithril]] produced by Mithril Minions does not give any skill xp.
  - [[Enchanted Mithril]] still gives skill xp.
- [[Prismarine Bricks]] placed by Mithril Minions are no longer custom blocks.
  - When mined by a player, the blocks will drop [[Prismarine Bricks]] rather than nothing with the message (see Trivia).
## Trivia
- Mithril Minions used to be bugged and not work while the [[Private Island]] wasn't loaded if it didn't contain any fuel.
- Mithril Minions have the third slowest base minion speed on Normal, [[Ironman]], and [[Bingo]] profiles. [[Vampire Minion|Vampire Minions]] and [[Inferno Minion|Inferno Minions]] are slower.
- Mithril Minions will place [[Prismarine Bricks]] around them to serve as [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]].
  - Mining the blocks manually will not drop [[Mithril]] and instead results in the following message: Drops from custom blocks are currently disabled on private islands!. This is likely because of a glitch that allowed minion-spawned [[Mithril|Mithril Ore]] to be mined much faster than intended.
  - This is currently bugged (see Bugs).
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Tier I-XII Mithril Minion.|
|Unknown Date|Mithril Minion blocks now drop [[Prismarine Bricks]] when mined by a player.|

# Mushroom Minion
Aliases: Mushroom Minion I, Mushroom Minion II, Mushroom Minion III, Mushroom Minion IV, Mushroom Minion IX, Mushroom Minion V, Mushroom Minion VI, Mushroom Minion VII, Mushroom Minion VIII, Mushroom Minion X, Mushroom Minion XI, Mushroom Minion XII

[Metadata]
ID: MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_1; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_2; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_3; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_4; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_5; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_6; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_7; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_8; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_9; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_10; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_11; MUSHROOM_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and harvesting red and brown mushrooms! Requires an area that is suitable for mushrooms to be placed. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 30s Max Storage: 128; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Mushroom Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants and collects [[Mushroom|Red Mushrooms]] and [[Mushroom|Brown Mushrooms]]. Mushroom Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Mushroom|Mushroom I (1)]].
Mushrooms planted by a Mushroom Minion are randomly a [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]] or a [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]].

|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 0.5; Chance of Obtaining: 50%|Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|+0.3 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 10 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 640 [[Coins]]|

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
A Mushroom Minion can place mushrooms on [[Mycelium]], [[Podzol]], [[Coarse Dirt]], and [[Dirt]] regardless of light level. Mushrooms can be placed on any full block[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] if the light level is lower than 13 for both sky and block light.

## Tips
- Upgrading a Mushroom Minion using only materials that it produces can be achieved by using one type of mushroom for the first ten tiers, and using the other type for tier XI. This minimizes unused materials.
## Trivia
- A Mushroom Minion can sometimes do a dance animation when in the dark. It is the only minion with this feature.
- If a Mushroom Minion cannot plant mushrooms due to no available space or the light level is too high, the Mushroom Minion will say No space or too much light ಠ_ಠ.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
A Mycelium Minion will place dirt to fill their spawning area. The minion can turn that dirt into mycelium if there is no mycelium and all the dirt has been placed. Because of this, the minion needs their spawning area to be clear of any other blocks when the [[Private Island]] is loaded (see #Bugs).
#### Special Layouts
A Mycelium Minion can be made almost twice as fast by having a ring of mycelium around the minion's spawning area. When mining mycelium, the minion converts it back to dirt instead of destroying it, so mycelium can naturally spread into their spawning area. This will cause the minion to skip the generating action. However, because the spreading is random, it is not consistently twice as fast. Make sure of the following when building this special layout:
- The spawning area is well-lit, as mycelium needs a light level of at least 12 in order to spread
- Ice melting from [[Ice Cube]] is turned on (/islandproperties), as turning it off stops random ticks which is needed for mycelium to spread.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

## Bugs
- If there is a block that is not air, dirt, or mycelium in a Mycelium Minion's spawning area, the minion will stop working when it runs out of mycelium and air because not all the dirt has been placed. This only happens while the island is loaded. The minion is not affected by this while offline.
## Trivia
- If a Mycelium Minion sees a player who is part of the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]], they will stop working and say Traitor!.

#### Special Layout
A Nether Wart Minion can be made twice as fast by placing a [[Nether Wart Crystal]] nearby. The [[Nether Wart Crystal]] auto-places grown nether wart. This will cause the minion to skip the planting action. However, the [[Nether Wart Crystal]] can service only 1 Nether Wart Minion.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

[Metadata]
ID: POTATO_GENERATOR_1; POTATO_GENERATOR_2; POTATO_GENERATOR_3; POTATO_GENERATOR_4; POTATO_GENERATOR_5; POTATO_GENERATOR_6; POTATO_GENERATOR_7; POTATO_GENERATOR_8; POTATO_GENERATOR_9; POTATO_GENERATOR_10; POTATO_GENERATOR_11; POTATO_GENERATOR_12

A Potato Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants and collects [[Potato|Potatoes]]. Potato Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Potato|Potato I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 3; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Potato]]|+0.1 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 3 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 192 [[Coins]]|
|[[Hunter Knife]]|1x [[Potato]]|[[French Fries]]||Per Item: NPC: 1 Coin ([[Coins]]), Stack: NPC: 64 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Potato]]|[[Enchanted Potato]]|+16 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 480 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 30,720 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Potato]]|[[Enchanted Baked Potato]]|+2,560 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 76,800 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 4,915,200 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
When a Pumpkin Minion has planted all the [[Pumpkin Seeds]], it will not have to perform more planting actions as [[Pumpkin|Pumpkins]] will grown automatically before each harvest. This only happens while the [[Private Island]] is loaded (see #Bugs).

- Offline calculations generate pumpkins twice as slow due to the simulation assuming a replant step is necessary after each harvest.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
Due to [[Red Sand]] being affected by gravity, there needs to be a block under the air to keep the sand in reach.
#### Special Layouts
A Red Sand Minion can be made almost twice as fast by letting the red sand fall on a non-full block (like [[Sign|Signs]] or [[Carpet]]) above a [[Hopper]]. The non-full block breaks the falling red sand and hoppers underneath can collect it. This will cause the minion to skip the mining action, but that does make the minion 10% slower[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], so it is almost twice as fast.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.
### Minion Modifiers
Some [[Minions|Minion Modifiers]] can only be applied to a limited selection of [[Minions]]. The following of which can be applied to a Red Sand Minion.
|Modifier Type|Affects Red Sand Minion|

|Upgrades ([[Minion Upgrades]])||

|[[Pets]]|[[Snail Pet]]|
|Mayors ([[Mayor Election]])|[[Cole]]|

|Modifier Type|Affects all [[Minions]]|

# Redstone Minion
Aliases: Redstone Minion I, Redstone Minion II, Redstone Minion III, Redstone Minion IV, Redstone Minion IX, Redstone Minion V, Redstone Minion VI, Redstone Minion VII, Redstone Minion VIII, Redstone Minion X, Redstone Minion XI, Redstone Minion XII, Redstone Minions
Categories: Minions, Craftable items, Redstone Dust Collection, Mining
[Redstone Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Redstone Dust I (1) ([[Redstone Dust]])
Category: [[Mining]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 3–6x [[Redstone Dust]]
Upgrade with: [[Redstone Dust]] [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]] [[Enchanted Redstone Block]]
[Metadata]
ID: REDSTONE_GENERATOR_1; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_2; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_3; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_4; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_5; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_6; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_7; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_8; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_9; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_10; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_11; REDSTONE_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and mining redstone ore!; Requires an open area to place redstone ore. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 29s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Redstone Minion is a [[Mining]] [[Minions|Minion]] that generates [[Ores|Redstone Ore]] and collects [[Redstone Dust]]. Redstone Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust I (1)]].
Redstone Minions are useful during early game because [[Redstone Dust|Redstone Dust Collection]] unlocks [[Accessory Bag]] upgrades.
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|

|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 4.5; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Redstone Dust]]|+0.2 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 1 Coin ([[Coins]]), Stack: NPC: 64 [[Coins]]|
|[[Compactor]]|9x [[Redstone Dust]]|[[Block of Redstone]]|+1.8 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 9 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 576 [[Coins]]|

|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]|[[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|+5,120 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 25,600 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 1,638,400 [[Coins]]|

# Revenant Minion
Aliases: Revenant Minion I, Revenant Minion II, Revenant Minion III, Revenant Minion IV, Revenant Minion IX, Revenant Minion V, Revenant Minion VI, Revenant Minion VII, Revenant Minion VIII, Revenant Minion X, Revenant Minion XI, Revenant Minion XII
Categories: Minions, Craftable items, Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Slayer Minions
[Revenant Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Zombie Slayer V (5) ([[Zombie Slayer]])
Category: [[Combat]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 2–5x [[Rotten Flesh]] 1x [[Diamond]](20%)
Upgrade with: [[Revenant Flesh]] [[Revenant Viscera]] [[Zombie Minion]] [[Revenant Minion XII Upgrade Stone]]
[Metadata]
ID: REVENANT_GENERATOR_1; REVENANT_GENERATOR_2; REVENANT_GENERATOR_3; REVENANT_GENERATOR_4; REVENANT_GENERATOR_5; REVENANT_GENERATOR_6; REVENANT_GENERATOR_7; REVENANT_GENERATOR_8; REVENANT_GENERATOR_9; REVENANT_GENERATOR_10; REVENANT_GENERATOR_11; REVENANT_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and struggling with Revenants! Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 29s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Revenant Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Revenant|Revenants]] and collects [[Rotten Flesh]] and [[Diamond|Diamonds]]. Revenant Minion recipes, except Revenant Minion XII, are unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer V (5)]]. Revenant Minion XII recipe is unlocked at [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer IX (9)]]. A Revenant Minion XII also needs [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer IX (9)]] to be placed down.
Players get one free Revenant Minion I by reaching [[Zombie Slayer|Zombie Slayer V (5)]].

|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 0.2; Chance of Obtaining: 20%|[[Diamond]]|+0.4 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 8 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 512 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Rotten Flesh]]|[[Enchanted Rotten Flesh]]|+48 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 320 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 20,480 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Diamond]]|[[Enchanted Diamond]]|+64 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 1,280 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 81,920 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Diamond]]|[[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|+10,240 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 204,800 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 13,107,200 [[Coins]]|

[Metadata]
ID: SAND_GENERATOR_1; SAND_GENERATOR_2; SAND_GENERATOR_3; SAND_GENERATOR_4; SAND_GENERATOR_5; SAND_GENERATOR_6; SAND_GENERATOR_7; SAND_GENERATOR_8; SAND_GENERATOR_9; SAND_GENERATOR_10; SAND_GENERATOR_11
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and mining sand! Requires an open area to place sand. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 26s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Sand Minion is a [[Mining]] [[Minions|Minion]] that generates and collects [[Sand]]. Sand Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Sand|Sand I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Sand]]|+0.2 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 2 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 128 [[Coins]]|
|[[Auto Smelter]]|1x [[Sand]]|[[Glass]]||Per Item: NPC: 2 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 128 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Sand]]|[[Enchanted Sand]]|+32 [[Mining]]|Per Item: NPC: 320 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 20,480 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.
### Stats

Note: No [[Minions|Minion Modifiers]] are used in the above profit calculation. Values shown are for when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.
### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
Due to [[Sand]] being affected by gravity, there needs to be a block under the air to keep the sand in reach.
#### Special Layouts
A Sand Minion can be made almost twice as fast by letting the sand fall on a non-full block (like [[Sign|Signs]] or [[Carpet]]) above a [[Hopper]]. The non-full block breaks the falling sand and hoppers underneath can collect it. This will cause the minion to skip the mining action, but that does make the minion 10% slower[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], so it is almost twice as fast.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

A Sheep Minion equipped with a pair of [[Enchanted Shears]] shears the sheep for more [[Wool]].

A Skeleton Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Skeleton|Skeletons]] and collects [[Bone|Bones]]. Skeleton Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Bone|Bone I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1.5; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Bone]]|+0.2 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 2 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 128 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Bone]]|[[Enchanted Bone]]|+32 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 320 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 20,480 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.

# Slime Minion
Aliases: Slime Minion I, Slime Minion II, Slime Minion III, Slime Minion IV, Slime Minion IX, Slime Minion V, Slime Minion VI, Slime Minion VII, Slime Minion VIII, Slime Minion X, Slime Minion XI, Slime Minion XII

Category: [[Combat]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1–3x [[Slimeball]]

[Metadata]
ID: SLIME_GENERATOR_1; SLIME_GENERATOR_2; SLIME_GENERATOR_3; SLIME_GENERATOR_4; SLIME_GENERATOR_5; SLIME_GENERATOR_6; SLIME_GENERATOR_7; SLIME_GENERATOR_8; SLIME_GENERATOR_9; SLIME_GENERATOR_10; SLIME_GENERATOR_11
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and slaying Slimes! Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 26s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Slime Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Slime (Minion Mob)|Slimes]] and collects [[Slimeball|Slimeballs]]. Slime Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Slimeball|Slimeball I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops

|-|-|-|-|-|

|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 2|[[Slimeball]]|+0.2 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 5 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 320 [[Coins]]|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 1 in 2|[[Slimeball]]|+0.2 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 5 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 320 [[Coins]]|
|[[Compactor]]|9x [[Slimeball]]|[[Slime Block]]|+1.8 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 45 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 2,880 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Slimeball]]|[[Enchanted Slimeball]]|+32 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 800 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 51,200 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Slimeball]]|[[Enchanted Slime Block]]|+5,120 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 128,000 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 8,192,000 [[Coins]]|

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
[[Slime (Minion Mob)|Slimes]] spawned by a Slime Minion come in 4 sizes: small, medium, semi-large, and large. For the minion to spawn a slime, there has to be enough space for the largest slime, which is 3x3x3.
### Minion Modifiers
Some [[Minions|Minion Modifiers]] can only be applied to a limited selection of [[Minions]]. The following of which can be applied to a Slime Minion.

|Crystals ([[Minion Crystal]])||
|[[Pets]]|[[Magma Cube Pet]]|
|Mayors ([[Mayor Election]])||
The following modifiers can be applied to all minions.
|Modifier Type|Affects all [[Minions]]|
|-|-|
|Fuel ([[Minion Fuel]])|[[Block of Coal]], [[Catalyst]], [[Charcoal]], [[Coal]], [[Enchanted Bread]], [[Enchanted Charcoal]], [[Enchanted Coal]], [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]], [[Everburning Flame]], [[Foul Flesh]], [[Hamster Wheel]], [[Hyper Catalyst]], [[Magma Bucket]], [[Plasma Bucket]], [[Solar Panel]], [[Tasty Cheese]]|

|Upgrades ([[Minion Upgrades]])|[[Diamond Spreading]], [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], [[Flycatcher]], [[Krampus Helmet]], [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]], [[Minion Expander]], [[Potato Spreading]], [[Sleepy Hollow]], [[Soulflow Engine]], [[Super Compactor 3000]]|
|Mayors ([[Mayor Election]])|[[Derpy]]|
|Misc.|[[Beacon]], [[Free Will]], [[Minion Storage]], [[Mithril Infusion]], [[Postcard]]|
### Stranded
Slimes that are spawned by a Slime Minion cannot transform into Magma Cubes through contact with lava.

## Tips
- It is recommended that players use [[Corrupt Soil]] with this minion as it effectively triples the money output.
## Trivia
- There used to be a bug where the minion could spawn slimes partially in a wall when the wall was on the opposite side of a chunk border. This happened because the check failed to cross the chunk border, either being void entirely or ending up on the opposite side of the original chunk depending on the orientation.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

|Oct 11, 2019|0.7.2|Slimes generated by the Slime Minion stop growing and instead, spawn at random sizes.|

## Tips
- Since it is a good and cheap minion for [[Mining|Mining XP]], it is recommended to have Snow Minions set up if the player does not want to mine manually.
- It is recommended to use a [[Minion Storage]] as a Snow Minion fills up fast, even with a [[Super Compactor 3000]].
- [[Diamond Spreading|Diamond Spreadings]] pair extremely well with a Snow Minion because of the multiple snowball drops. The [[Diamond|Diamonds]] provide more [[Mining|Mining XP]] and extra profit.
- Using a Snow Minion XI with a [[Super Compactor 3000]], [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]], and [[Diamond Spreading]], the Snow Minion generates around 60,000 [[Coins]] per day.
  - Players who don't check the minion often enough should consider using [[Budget Hopper|Budget Hoppers]] or [[Enchanted Hopper|Enchanted Hoppers]] since they automatically sell extra minion outputs for a portion of the NPC sell price (50% for [[Budget Hopper]], 70% for [[Enchanted Hopper]]).
## Trivia
- The base actions per second of a Snow Minion XII is the fastest of all minions.
- Before the Bazaar Update, Snow Minions were very often used for money. It was cheap and good for selling to [[Shops|NPC Shops]], which was quicker and easier than selling materials to the auction house. However, since then, the bazaar makes it easier to sell materials with high demand at higher prices. Additionally, a lot of buffs and [[Minions|Minion Modifiers]] have been added that make other minions significantly better than a Snow Minion in terms of profitability.
- The [[Mining|Mining XP]] production of a Snow Minion is the second highest, which was really useful before the introduction of [[Dwarven Mines]], since manually grinding mining without a good mining gear or without [[Cole]] as [[Mayor Election|mayor]] with 1.5x [[Mining|Mining XP]] on public islands used to be extremely inefficient.
  - An [[Ice Minion]] can reach higher [[Mining|Mining XP]] production rates.

# Sugar Cane Minion
Aliases: Sugar Cane Minion I, Sugar Cane Minion II, Sugar Cane Minion III, Sugar Cane Minion IV, Sugar Cane Minion IX, Sugar Cane Minion V, Sugar Cane Minion VI, Sugar Cane Minion VII, Sugar Cane Minion VIII, Sugar Cane Minion X, Sugar Cane Minion XI, Sugar Cane Minion XII

[Sugar Cane Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Sugar Cane I (1) ([[Sugar Cane]])
Category: [[Farming]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 3x [[Sugar Cane]]
Upgrade with: [[Sugar Cane]] [[Enchanted Sugar]] [[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]
[Metadata]
ID: SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_1; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_2; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_3; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_4; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_5; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_6; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_7; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_8; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_9; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_10; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_11; SUGAR_CANE_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and harvesting sugar canes! Requires an area that is suitable for sugar cane to be placed. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 22s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Sugar Cane Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants and collects [[Sugar Cane]]. Sugar Cane Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Sugar Cane|Sugar Cane I (1)]].
## Usage
### Drops
|Condition|Input|Output|XP|Sell Price|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 3; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Sugar Cane]]|+0.1 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC (Price when sold to a shop.): 4 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 256 [[Coins]]|
|[[Super Compactor 3000]]|160x [[Sugar Cane]]|[[Enchanted Sugar]]|+16 [[Alchemy]]|Per Item: NPC: 640 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 40,960 [[Coins]]|
|(same as above)|160x [[Enchanted Sugar]]|[[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]|+2,560 [[Farming]]|Per Item: NPC: 102,400 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 6,553,600 [[Coins]]|
Note: [[Auto Smelter]] and [[Super Compactor 3000]] mentioned above are interchangeable with [[Dwarven Super Compactor]], which can perform the combination of functionalities of the prior two items.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
A Sugar Cane Minion can place sugar cane on [[Grass]], [[Dirt]], [[Coarse Dirt]], [[Podzol]], [[Sand]], or [[Red Sand]] that are next to water.

## Bugs
- A Sugar Cane Minion suffers reduced rates if the [[Private Island]] is loaded. This is because the Sugar Cane Minion cannot identify if the sugar canes are fully grown. Meaning the minion can break 1-block tall sugar cane alongside 2 & 3-block tall sugar cane, which makes the harvest not always give 3 [[Sugar Cane]]. The minion generates the proper amount of resources while offline.

# Sunflower Minion
Aliases: Moonflower Minion, Sunflower Minion I, Sunflower Minion II, Sunflower Minion III, Sunflower Minion IV, Sunflower Minion IX, Sunflower Minion V, Sunflower Minion VI, Sunflower Minion VII, Sunflower Minion VIII, Sunflower Minion X, Sunflower Minion XI, Sunflower Minion XII

[Metadata]
ID: SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_1; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_2; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_3; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_4; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_5; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_6; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_7; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_8; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_9; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_10; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_11; SUNFLOWER_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating sunflowers during the day, and moonflowers at night.; Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 24s Max Storage: 128; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Sunflower Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants and collects [[Sunflower|Sunflowers]] during the day and [[Moonflower|Moonflowers]] at night. Sunflower Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Sunflower|Sunflower II (2)]].
A Sunflower Minion can be given a [[Dayswitch]] or [[Nightswitch]] to gain a 20% speed boost and produce [[Sunflower]] or [[Moonflower]] respectively at all times.

# Tarantula Minion
Aliases: Tarantula Minion I, Tarantula Minion II, Tarantula Minion III, Tarantula Minion IV, Tarantula Minion IX, Tarantula Minion V, Tarantula Minion VI, Tarantula Minion VII, Tarantula Minion VIII, Tarantula Minion X, Tarantula Minion XI, Tarantula Minion XII

[Tarantula Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Spider Slayer V (5) ([[Spider Slayer]])
Category: [[Combat]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 2–5x [[String]] 1x [[Spider Eye]] 1x [[Iron Ingot]](20%)
Upgrade with: [[Tarantula Web]] [[Tarantula Silk]] [[Spider Minion]] [[Tarantula Minion XII Upgrade Stone]]
[Metadata]
ID: TARANTULA_GENERATOR_1; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_2; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_3; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_4; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_5; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_6; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_7; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_8; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_9; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_10; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_11; TARANTULA_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and squashing Tarantulas! Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 29s Max Storage: 64; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Tarantula Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Tarantula Spider|Tarantula Spiders]] and collects [[String]], [[Spider Eye|Spider Eyes]], and [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]]. Tarantula Minion recipes, except Tarantula Minion XII, are unlocked at [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer V (5)]]. Tarantula Minion XII recipe is unlocked at [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer IX (9)]]. A Tarantula Minion XII does not need [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer IX (9)]] to be placed down.
Players get one free Tarantula Minion I by reaching [[Spider Slayer|Spider Slayer V (5)]].

### Drops

|-|-|-|-|-|

|None|From Harvest Avg. Amount: 1; Chance of Obtaining: 100%|[[Spider Eye]]|+0.3 [[Combat]]|Per Item: NPC: 3 [[Coins]], Stack: NPC: 192 [[Coins]]|

## Trivia
- Vampire Minion and [[Revenant Minion]] are the only minion types that require armor to craft their tier I.
- The item held by the Vampire Minion changes depending on its tier.
  - Tiers 1 to 3 hold a [[Self-Recursive Pickaxe]], Tiers 4 to 6 hold a [[Silver-Laced Karambit]], and Tiers 7 to 11 hold a [[Steak Stake]].

A Voidling Minion is a [[Combat]] [[Minions|Minion]] that spawns [[Voidling|Voidlings]] and collects [[Obsidian]], [[Nether Quartz]], and [[Enchanted Ender Pearl|Enchanted Ender Pearls]]. Voidling Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Enderman Slayer|Enderman Slayer IV (4)]].
A Voidling Minion equipped with a [[Soulflow Engine]] gives the upgrade a 3% per minion tier increase in the production of [[Raw Soulflow]].

## Trivia
- Voidling Minion was released before a [[Wolf Slayer]] specific minion type, while [[Enderman Slayer]] was released after [[Wolf Slayer]].
- While a [[Revenant Minion]] and a [[Tarantula Minion]] produce 2–5 of the main drop of their respective mobs, a Voidling Minion produces 2–3 [[Obsidian]].
- Voidling Minion is the only minion type that can directly harvest an enchanted material.
- A Voidling Minion has a chance to say special dialogue, such as "Throw me your summoning eyes and I'll place them!" or "I got 9/9/9 before atoned" in white.
- Voidling Minion is one of the most costly minion types to upgrade. It is outpriced by [[Tarantula Minion]] and [[Inferno Minion]].
- A Voidling Minion produces mostly mining materials. Despite this, it is not considered a [[Mining]] Minion and its speed is not buffed by a [[Mithril Crystal]] or [[Cole]]'s Prospection perk.

# Wheat Minion
Aliases: Wheat Minion I, Wheat Minion II, Wheat Minion III, Wheat Minion IV, Wheat Minion IX, Wheat Minion V, Wheat Minion VI, Wheat Minion VII, Wheat Minion VIII, Wheat Minion X, Wheat Minion XI, Wheat Minion XII
Categories: Minions, More info needed, Craftable items, Wheat Collection, Farming, Farming Minions
[Wheat Minion]
Type: Minion
Collection: Wheat I (1) ([[Wheat]])
Category: [[Farming]] Minions
Collects (Range and chance of items collected per harvest action): 1x [[Wheat]] 1–2x [[Seeds]]
Upgrade with: [[Wheat]] [[Enchanted Wheat]]
[Metadata]
ID: WHEAT_GENERATOR_1; WHEAT_GENERATOR_2; WHEAT_GENERATOR_3; WHEAT_GENERATOR_4; WHEAT_GENERATOR_5; WHEAT_GENERATOR_6; WHEAT_GENERATOR_7; WHEAT_GENERATOR_8; WHEAT_GENERATOR_9; WHEAT_GENERATOR_10; WHEAT_GENERATOR_11; WHEAT_GENERATOR_12
Tooltip: Place this minion and it will start generating and harvesting wheat.; Requires dirt or soil nearby so wheat can be planted. Minions also work when you are offline!; Time Between Action: 15s Max Storage: 128; Resources Generated: #; RARE
A Wheat Minion is a [[Farming]] [[Minions|Minion]] that plants and collects [[Wheat]] and [[Seeds]]. Wheat Minion recipes are unlocked at [[Wheat|Wheat I (1)]]
A Wheat Minion V is located in the [[Farm]] of the [[Hub Island]]. It has an [[Enchanted Bread]] as [[Minion Fuel]] and a [[Budget Hopper]] as [[Automated Shipping]]. Players can only collect the resources from the minion's storage. [[Arthur]] stops the player if they try anything else with the minion.

### Layouts
#### Ideal Layout
The following is the standard ideal layout as shown in-game in the minion's menu.
#### Special Layouts
A Wheat Minion can be made twice as fast by placing a [[Wheat Crystal]] nearby. The [[Wheat Crystal]] auto-places grown wheat. This will cause the minion to skip the planting action. However, with too many speed boosts[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; What is the boundary?: see comment in source code; When this info is added, remove this.), the crystal and the minion get out of sync, resulting in only a fraction of the planting actions of the minion getting skipped. Furthermore, the [[Wheat Crystal]] can service only 1 Wheat Minion.
Special layouts only work when the [[Private Island]] is loaded.

## Trivia
- Before [[Enchanted Wheat]] and [[Enchanted Hay Bale|Enchanted Hay Bales]] were added. [[Wheat]] could be compacted to [[Enchanted Bread]] in a Wheat Minion.
- The tier of the Wheat Minion in the [[Hub Island]] used to be upgradable by players.
  - Server restarts would reset the minion to tier V.

## Trivia
- In [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] mode, a Zombie Minion can hold a [[Sea Lantern Hat]] in their upgrade slots to spawn [[Lapis Zombie|Lapis Zombies]], which is the only way to obtain [[Lapis Lazuli]] in [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]].

# Consumables
Aliases: Consumable, Permanent Consumable, Permanent Consumables, Temporary Consumable
Categories: Mob Drops, More info needed, Consumables
[Consumables]
Consumables are items that can be consumed to gain a permanent or temporary effect.
## List of Consumables
### Permanent
Permanent Consumables are items that once used give a permanent [[Stats|Stat]] increase or unique perks, alongside [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].

### Temporary
Temporary Consumables are items that once used give a temporary [[Stats|stat]] increase or unique perks and are not affected by duration enhancers, for example, [[Alchemy]]. The term of "Temporary Consumable" is improperly defined to differentiate them from Permanent Consumables, although [[Mixins]] and [[Potions]] could have been included in this definition.

# Crimson Essence
Categories: Mob Drops, Pages with UIs
[Crimson Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Crimson Essence]] Crimson Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Obtaining
[[Crimson Essence]] Crimson Essence can be be obtained in the [[Crimson Isle]] in multiple ways:
- Opening chests from [[Kuudra]] runs.
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Crimson Essence]] Crimson Essence.
- Killing certain mobs:
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv508]|[[Ashfang]]|50|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv523]|[[Magma Boss]]|50|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv486]|[[Barbarian Duke X]]|25|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv477]|[[Bladesoul]]|25|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[Lv515]|[[Mage Outlaw]]|25|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
### Increasing Crimson Essence Gain
There are several sources that can be used to increase Crimson Essence gained:
|Icon|Name|Type|Effect|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Kuudra Pet]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])+ [[Kuudra Pet]]|Pet ([[Pets]])|Gain +15%–20% more [[Crimson Essence]] Crimson Essence.|
|[[Kuudra Pet]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])+ [[Kuudra Pet]]|Pet ([[Pets]])|(same as above)|
|[[Kuudra Pet]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])+ [[Kuudra Pet]]|Pet ([[Pets]])|(same as above)|
|[[Lava Leech Shard]]|Essence of Crimson ([[Attributes]])|Shard ([[Attributes]])|Gain +1%–13% more [[Crimson Essence]] Crimson Essence.|
|[[Komodo Dragon Shard]]|Echo of Essence ([[Attributes]])|Shard ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
|[[Tiamat Shard]]|Echo of Echoes ([[Attributes]])|Shard ([[Attributes]])|(same as above)|
## Usage
### Star Upgrades

# Diamond Essence
Categories: Mob Drops, Pages with UIs
[Diamond Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Diamond Essence]] Diamond Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Obtaining
[[Diamond Essence]] Diamond Essence can be be obtained in the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], and the [[Catacombs]] in multiple ways:
- Opening [[Crystal Hollows|Crystal Hollows Treasure Chests]].
- Completing the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
- [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret Chests]]
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Diamond Essence]] Diamond Essence.
- Unlocking [[Diamond Heads]] in Boss Collections.
- Unlocking [[Gemma]]'s Shop.
- Trading with [[Gemma]] visitor in [[The Garden]].
### Mob Drops

[[Dragon Essence]] Dragon Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in [[The End]].
## Obtaining
[[Dragon Essence]] Dragon Essence can be be obtained in [[The End]] and the [[Catacombs]] in multiple ways:
- Sacrificing specific items to the [[Draconic Altar]].
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Dragon Essence]] Dragon Essence.
- Trading with [[Tyzzo]] visitor in [[The Garden]].
- Killing certain mobs:

# Endcaps
Aliases: Endcap, Endcap Enchantment, Endcap Enchantments, Endcap Items, Endcap Upgrade, Endcap Upgrades, Endcap enchantment, Endcap enchantments, Endpoint, Endpoint Enchantment, Endpoint Enchantments, Endpoint Items, Endpoint enchantment, Endpoint enchantments
Categories: Mob Drops, Endcap Enchantments
[Endcaps]
Endcaps are items that upgrade an [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] to a higher tier, requiring its respective [[Enchantments|Enchantment]]'s immediately preceding tier to be applied. They can be combined with items in an [[Anvil]].
Most Endcap Upgrades can also be obtained from the [[Experimentation Table]], with the exception of those obtained from crafting.

Aliases: Endermen Pet, Endermen pet

Pet ID: ENDERMAN

The Enderman Pet is a [[Combat]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
The Enderman Pet can be obtained by killing [[Enderman|Endermen]] or [[Voidling Fanatic|Voidling Fanatics]] in [[The End]]. The possible rarities depend on the mob's level.

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]||1 Day|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|50k [[Coins]]||2 Days|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|100k [[Coins]]||6 Days|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|40M [[Coins]]|8x [[Enchanted Eye of Ender]]|12 Days|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) → MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1M [[Coins]]|1x [[Enderman Cortex Rewriter]]|1 hour|

## Tips
- The Enderman Pet is a useful pet for early game, due to its cheap [[Auction House]] price and relatively common drop rate. It provides great damage for people who can't spend a lot of money or are [[Ironman]].
  - The [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] rarity does not provide a higher damage buff, therefore it may be better to get a [[Rarity|COMMON]] pet, as it levels faster, unless the player wants the [[Rarity|RARE]], [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]], or [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] perks.
  - While Teleport Savvy no longer applies when swapping pets, the ability still grants a significant damage bonus while the pet is active.
- It is recommended to sell non-[[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Enderman Pets to [[George]] as they sell for a very low amount on the [[Auction House]] due to how many Enderman Pets are in circulation.
## Trivia
- The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Enderman Pet is one of 5 pets designed for its respective [[Slayer]]: the others being the [[Ghoul Pet]] for [[Zombie Slayer]], the [[Tarantula Pet]] for [[Spider Slayer]], the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Hound Pet]] for [[Wolf Slayer]], and the [[Wisp Pet]] for [[Blaze Slayer]].
- The Teleport Savvy ability buffs the [[Damage]] of all weapons with a teleportation ability (e.g. [[Sinseeker Scythe]], [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], etc) and is not limited to the [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Aspect of the Void]].
- The Enderman Pet used to be swapped to for the Teleport Savvy ability bonus, before being swapped out with pet rules. This behavior has since been patched.
- The first [[Pet Skins|pet skin]] in the game, the [[Spooky Enderman Skin]], is an Enderman Pet skin.

## Obtaining
The Flying Fish Pet has a chance to drop from [[The Loch Emperor]], which requires [[Fishing|Fishing XX (20)]] to be fished up. If a [[Flying Fish Pet]] Flying Fish Pet has dropped, another pet of a different rarity cannot drop.

## Trivia
- There used to be a bug where you could get multiple Flying Fish Pets from the same [[The Loch Emperor|Sea Emperor]] (the Loch Emperor's old name) by using an explosive ability like Ink Bomb or [[Explosive Bow]].
- The Water Bender ability might be a reference to Avatar: The Last Airbender or the Indonesian empire of Srivijaya that was famous for its ships dubbed the "Water benders".
- This pet and the [[Scatha Pet]] are the only two pets with their lowest rarity being [[Rarity|RARE]].
- The Flying Fish Pet may be a reference to the Terraria mob of the same name.
- The Flying Fish Pet is a recoloration of the Pokémon Magikarp.
- The texture for the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Flying Fish Pet may be a reference to Pokémon Uranium, a Pokémon fangame that features various Pokémons corrupted by radiation, including Magikarp.
- The Flying Fish Pet is the only [[Pets|Pet]] whose perks get completely revamped at [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity.
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Flying Fish Pet does not buff the [[Treasure Chance]] from the Tiered Bonus of [[Diver's Armor]].
## History

|Feb 21, 2020|0.7.6|Added Flying Fish Pet.|
|May 5, 2022|Flying Fish Pet can be upgraded to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) rarity with a [[Radioactive Vial]].|
|Aug 18, 2022|0.14|Quick Reel now grants 0.75 [[Fishing Speed]] per level at LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) rarity.|
|Sep 28, 2022|0.15|Quick Reel now grants 0.8 [[Fishing Speed]] per level at LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) and MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) rarity.|
|Aug 1, 2023|0.19.2|Fixed typo in Flying Fish Pet's Rapid Decay perk description.|

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|5M [[Coins]]|512x (8 stacks (512 items total)) [[Revenant Flesh]]|10 Days|
## Skins
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Obtaining|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Ghoulfriend Ghoul Skin]]|[[Ghoulfriend Ghoul Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Fall 2024 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- Despite the Ghoul Pet no longer granting a multiplicative [[Combat|Combat XP]] buff against Zombies, it still gives more [[Combat Wisdom]] against Zombies than the [[Wolf Pet]].
## Trivia
- The Ghoul Pet is one of 5 pets designed for its respective [[Slayer]]: the others being the [[Tarantula Pet]] for [[Spider Slayer]], the [[Hound Pet]] for [[Wolf Slayer]], the [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Enderman Pet]] for [[Enderman Slayer]], and the [[Wisp Pet]] for [[Blaze Slayer]].

# Gold Essence
Categories: Mob Drops, Pages with UIs
[Gold Essence]
Type: [[Currency]]
[[Gold Essence]] Gold Essence is an [[Essence]]. It is primarily used in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Obtaining
[[Gold Essence]] Gold Essence can be be obtained in the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], and the [[Catacombs]] in multiple ways:
- Opening [[Crystal Hollows|Crystal Hollows Treasure Chests]].
- Completing the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
- [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret Chests]]
- Killing [[The Watcher|Watcher Undeads]] with the Golden modifier in the [[Catacombs]].
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Gold Essence]] Gold Essence.
- Unlocking [[Golden Heads]] in Boss Collections.
- Unlocking [[Marigold]]'s Shop.
- Trading with [[Gold Forger]] and [[Marigold]] visitors in [[The Garden]].

## Obtaining
[[Ice Essence]] Ice Essence can be be obtained in [[Jerry's Workshop]] and the [[Catacombs]] in multiple ways:
- [[Dungeon Secrets|Dungeon Secret Chests]]
- [[Salvaging]] certain items and items upgraded with [[Ice Essence]] Ice Essence.
- Trading with [[Frozen Alex]] visitor in [[The Garden]].
- [[Treasure|Treasure Fishing]] in [[Jerry's Workshop]].

# Kuudra Keys
Aliases: Burning Kuudra Key, Fiery Kuudra Key, Hot Kuudra Key, Infernal Kuudra Key, Kuudra Key
Categories: Mob Drops, Items, Craftable items, Kuudra
[Kuudra Keys]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])–LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Enchantable: ✕ No
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✕ No; (Except to Co-op members)
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Soulbound: ✔ Yes (Co-op)
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KUUDRA_TIER_KEY; KUUDRA_HOT_TIER_KEY; KUUDRA_BURNING_TIER_KEY; KUUDRA_FIERY_TIER_KEY; KUUDRA_INFERNAL_TIER_KEY
Tooltip: Used to unlock the reward chest in the Basic Tier of the Kuudra fight.; Right-click to view recipes!; * Co-op Soulbound * RARE
Kuudra Keys are [[Rarity|RARE]], [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] items that can be purchased from the Mage or Barbarian Emissary. It allows players to unlock the reward chest in the [[Kuudra]] fight of their respective tier. Kuudra Keys are automatically [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]] upon obtaining.
## Obtaining
### Shop Purchase
Kuudra Keys can be purchased from the [[Mage Emissary]] or [[Barbarian Emissary]]. Higher tiers have [[Factions|Reputation]] requirements to buy them.
|Slot|Name|[[Barbarian Emissary]]|[[Mage Emissary]]|Reputation ([[Factions]]) Requirement|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Kuudra Key|Kuudra Key|2x [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 200,000 [[Coins]]|2x [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 200,000 [[Coins]]||
|Hot Kuudra Key|Hot Kuudra Key|6x [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 400,000 [[Coins]]|6x [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 400,000 [[Coins]]|1,000 Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|Burning Kuudra Key|Burning Kuudra Key|20x [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 750,000 [[Coins]]|20x [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 750,000 [[Coins]]|3,000 Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|Fiery Kuudra Key|Fiery Kuudra Key|60x [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 1,500,000 [[Coins]]|60x [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 1,500,000 [[Coins]]|7,000 Reputation ([[Factions]])|
|Infernal Kuudra Key|Infernal Kuudra Key|120x (1 stack plus 56 (120 in total)) [[Enchanted Red Sand]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 3,000,000 [[Coins]]|120x [[Enchanted Mycelium]] 2x [[Nether Star]] 3,000,000 [[Coins]]|12,000 Reputation ([[Factions]])|
### Mob Drops
Kuudra Keys and Hot Kuudra Keys are dropped from a [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]: [[Bladesoul]], [[Mage Outlaw]], [[Ashfang]], [[Barbarian Duke X]].
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv523]|[[Magma Boss]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|12%|
|[Lv508]|[[Ashfang]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|6%|
|[Lv486]|[[Barbarian Duke X]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|6%|
|[Lv477]|[[Bladesoul]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|6%|
|[Lv515]|[[Mage Outlaw]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|6%|
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv523]|[[Magma Boss]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|8%|
|[Lv508]|[[Ashfang]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|4%|
|[Lv486]|[[Barbarian Duke X]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|4%|
|[Lv477]|[[Bladesoul]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|4%|
|[Lv515]|[[Mage Outlaw]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|4%|
### Fishing
All tiers of Kuudra Keys can be obtained as GREAT CATCH [[Treasure]] loot from lava fishing on the [[Crimson Isle]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Hot Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]])|None|4x Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]])|Recipe: [[Kuudra Keys]] ×4 → [[Kuudra Keys]]|
|Burning Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]])|None|4x Hot Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]])|Recipe: [[Kuudra Keys]] ×4 → [[Kuudra Keys]]|

|Infernal Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]])|None|4x Fiery Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]])|Recipe: [[Kuudra Keys]] ×4 → [[Kuudra Keys]]|
## Usage
Kuudra Keys allow players to unlock the reward chest in the [[Kuudra]] fight of the key's respective tier.
